- **climate-risk-underwriter**: Prompt playbook for a climate risk underwriter. Use when a climate risk underwriter wants help with work tasks such as: submission triage with climate lens; explain cat model output differences; forward-looking loading rationale; parametric trigger design review; regulatory climate disclosure draft; non-renewal or rate increase explanation; portfolio accumulation and climate stress plan; climate science upskilling plan. ([avivelbag/future-career-prompt-skills](https://github.com/avivelbag/future-career-prompt-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/climate-risk-underwriter))
- **climate-safe-service-area-analysis**: Compute reachable areas within a time limit that also stay outside extreme heat, flood-hazard or disaster boundaries. Use this skill whenever the user mentions climate safe, flood hazard, heat exposure, disaster boundary, hazard avoidance, safe reachability, resilience planning, or is working with UNICEF GeoSight, Valhalla, NOAA, even if they never say "climate-safe service area analysis" explicitly. Routes live lookups through the Climate-Safe-Routing-API endpoint. Do not use it for unrelated application feature work or general coding questions. ([Zero-Human-Corp/the-skills-barn](https://github.com/Zero-Human-Corp/the-skills-barn/tree/HEAD/skills/geospatial-mapping/climate-safe-service-area-analysis))

- **climate-science**: Climate physics, forcing, feedback, and attribution. Covers the greenhouse effect and radiative forcing, climate sensitivity, fast and slow feedbacks, the paleoclimate record, detection and attribution science, regional climate impacts, and the distinction between weather, climate variability, and forced climate change. Use when reasoning about global warming mechanisms, interpreting temperature or CO2 records, evaluating attribution claims for extreme events, or distinguishing signal from natural variability. ([Tibsfox/gsd-skill-creator](https://github.com/Tibsfox/gsd-skill-creator/tree/HEAD/examples/skills/environmental/climate-science) · ★71 · 2 copies)
- **climate-science**: Climate data analysis using xarray, netCDF4, xESMF, intake-esm, Pangeo, dask, cartopy, and regionmask. Use when reading CMIP6/CESM/ERA5 NetCDF files, computing climatologies and anomalies, regridding between grids, applying CF conventions, chunking with dask on the cloud, plotting maps in Robinson or Orthographic projections, masking regions, or running extreme value analysis with scipy.genextreme. ([Pratikrishi97/sciagent-skills](https://github.com/Pratikrishi97/sciagent-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/climate-science))
